00205: def owner_meshes(slot):

00211: 
00212: def scoped_materials(slot, callback):
00213:     for obj in owner_meshes(slot):
00214:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00215:         for i, material in enumerate(obj.data.materials):
00216:             if material:

00219:                 m.use_nodes = True
00220:                 shader = m.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00221:                 if shader:
00222:                     callback(shader, m)

00206: def owner_meshes(slot):
00207:     marker = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00208:     assert marker is not None, "Missing semantic marker: "+slot
00209:     result = [o for o in marker.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00210:     if marker.type == 'MESH':
00211:         result.append(marker)
00212:     assert result, "No meshes under "+slot
00213:     return result
00214: 
00215: def scoped_materials(slot, callback):
00216:     for obj in owner_meshes(slot):
00217:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00218:         for i, material in enumerate(obj.data.materials):
00219:             if material:
00220:                 m = material.copy()
00221:                 obj.data.materials[i] = m
00222:                 m.use_nodes = True
00223:                 shader = m.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00224:                 if shader:
00225:                     callback(shader, m)

00172: def mesh_obj(name,verts,faces,mat):
00173:     me = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00174:     me.from_pydata(verts,[],faces)
00175:     me.update()
00176:     ob = bpy.data.objects.new(name,me)
00177:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00178:     ob.data.materials.append(mat)
00179:     for p in me.polygons:

00181:     return ob

00183: def tube(name,points,radius,mat):
00184:     cu = bpy.data.curves.new(name,"CURVE")
00185:     cu.dimensions = "3D"
00186:     cu.resolution_u = 2
00187:     cu.bevel_depth = radius
00188:     cu.bevel_resolution = 2
00189:     sp = cu.splines.new("POLY")
00190:     sp.points.add(len(points)-1)
00191:     for p,co in zip(sp.points,points):
00192:         p.co = (*co,1)
00193:     ob = bpy.data.objects.new(name,cu)
00194:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00195:     ob.data.materials.append(mat)
00196:     return ob

00198: def signedpow(v,p):
00199:     return math.copysign(abs(v)**p,v)
